# Title: AdBlock DNSMasq servers Lite
# Description: 适用于 DNSMasq servers 的去广告合并规则，每 12 小时更新一次。规则源：AdGuard Base filter、AdGuard Chinese filter、AdGuard Mobile Ads filter、AdGuard DNS filter、jiekouAD、AWAvenue Ads Rule、anti-AD、HalfLife、DD-AD、晴雅广告拦截规则、SMAdHosts、那个谁520规则、TTDNS、茯苓广告规则、HG、DNS-Kuner拦截列表、Cats-Team/AdRules规则、SmartTV、HaGeZi's Apple Tracker Blocklist、HaGeZi's Xiaomi Tracker Blocklist、HaGeZi's Vivo Tracker Blocklist、HaGeZi's Samsung Tracker Blocklist、HaGeZi's OPPO & Realme Tracker Blocklist、茯苓允许列表、DD-AD允许列表、那个谁520广告白名单、DNS-Kuner放行列表。Lite 版仅针对国内域名拦截。
